(dynamic workflow)","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"design","description":"Grant or revoke Claude agent access to your Design projects","argumentHint":"consent | revoke","builtin":true},{"name":"design-sync","description":"Push a React design system to claude.ai/design. This runs a converter that bundles the real component code (from Storybook or a bare package) and uploads it. Use when the user runs /design-sync or says \"sync my design system to Claude Design\".","argumentHint":"[<project hint, e.g. \"Acme DS\">]","builtin":true},{"name":"dataviz","description":"Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. When the destination is a first-party document connector (host-designated, never self-described) that renders live charts, hand it the rows (inline, or as an uploaded data file the chart cites) rather than a rendered PNG/SVG — a picture of a chart loses hover, data inspection and per-value comments. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"update-config","description":"Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"verify","description":"Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"debug","description":"Enable debug logging for this session and help diagnose issues","argumentHint":"[issue description]","builtin":true},{"name":"code-review","description":"Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs (plus reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups where the model's review recipe covers them) at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review. For ultra on a GitHub.com PR target, --post asks to post the finished review’s findings to the PR as a single comment from the user’s GitHub account (not a review; the launch dialog still confirms in interactive sessions, while non-interactive mode posts on the flag alone) and --no-post hides that option.","argumentHint":"[low|medium|high|xhigh|max|ultra] [--fix] [--comment] [<pr#>|<branch>|<path>]","aliases":["review"],"builtin":true},{"name":"simplify","description":"Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.","argumentHint":"[<target>]","builtin":true},{"name":"batch","description":"Research and plan a large-scale change, then execute it in parallel across 5–30 isolated worktree agents that each open a PR.","argumentHint":"<instruction>","builtin":true},{"name":"fewer-permission-prompts","description":"Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"doctor","description":"Health-check the user's Claude Code setup and fix issues: diagnose installation health — what the `claude doctor` terminal diagnostics cover — from local data (duplicate or leftover installs, PATH, unparseable settings files, broken or colliding agent definitions, skills whose frontmatter fails to parse); find unused skills, MCP servers, and plugins versus their context cost and disable dead weight; deduplicate local CLAUDE.md files against checked-in ones; trim checked-in CLAUDE.md files by cutting content a session could derive from the codebase (directory layouts, tech-stack lists, architecture overviews) while keeping gotchas, rationale, and non-standard conventions; migrate always-loaded CLAUDE.md guidance into lazy skills and nested CLAUDE.md files; flag slow hooks and context-heavy extensions; check the installed version is current; make auto mode the default permission mode; and pre-approve frequently denied read-only commands. Use when the user asks for a doctor run, checkup, audit, tune-up, or cleanup of their Claude Code setup or configuration.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["checkup"],"builtin":true},{"name":"loop","description":"Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Claude desktop app (also called the in-app browser, the browser pane, Claude's browser, or \"your own browser\"), whose tools are named mcp__Claude_Browser__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser tool instead. It covers the pane's persistent sign-ins, tabs and preview_start, reading pages as text, site approvals, what the pane cannot open, and what to do when it cannot be reached. It is not for Claude in Chrome (mcp__claude-in-chrome__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["built-in-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:chrome-browser","description":"Read this skill before the first step that uses Claude in Chrome, the browser extension whose tools are named mcp__claude-in-chrome__* (also called Chrome, the browser extension, or the external browser) and which acts in the person's real Chrome with their own sign-ins; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__claude-in-chrome tool instead. It covers loading the tools in one ToolSearch call, checking the person's open tabs and working in a new tab, site permissions, GIF recordings, console logs, dialogs to avoid, and when to stop and ask. It is not for the built-in browser (mcp__Claude_Browser__* or mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["chrome-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:computer-use","description":"Read this skill before the first step of any request to do something in an app on the person's own computer (Notes, Finder, System Settings, any desktop app), to look at their screen, or for \"computer use\". Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["computer-use"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:deep-research","description":"Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":""},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docs","description":"docs (living docs people share, comment on and edit; use only when the user asks for one: names a doc, document, page, memo, spec, PRD, runbook or write-up, asks for somewhere to share or keep editing something, or says yes to your doc offer; a plan, comparison, summary or notes asked in chat stays in chat (at most a one-line doc offer); a report, status update, recap or \"something I can send them\" with no form named → ask first: reply, doc or file?; tabs hold tables and live charts too; a pasted claude.ai/code/artifact/… link may be a doc: check with docs tools first; not HTML pages, apps or plain chat answers; a .docx/.pptx/.xlsx/PDF asked for by name → that format's skill): asked for one → no docs-connector instructions in context? call the docs connector's `guide` with topic.instructions first, then create the doc (headings only, no body) before any search, file read or plan, even with files attached. Documenting code means docstrings or repo docs, not a doc. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docs"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docx","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx) or Word templates (.dotx). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file (to download, email or print), use this skill. However, if they ask for a document, page, report, memo, or notes WITHOUT naming a file format and the session offers Claude's own dedicated document or page skill or connector, use that instead. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or coding unrelated to document generation. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:import-memory","description":"Import a memory export from another AI assistant into Claude's memory — conversationally, additively, and with the content treated as data. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["import-memory"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:morning","description":"Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["morning"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pdf","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pdf"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pptx","description":"Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations as PowerPoint (.pptx) files; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email, summary, or creating a different type of slide deck); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user asks for a PowerPoint or .pptx file, or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. However, when the user asks for a deck, slides, a slide deck, or a presentation without naming a file format, default to using a dedicated slide-deck artifact type or a separate slides skill if this session offers one; otherwise, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pptx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:setup-writing-style","description":"Learns how the user writes from their own sent messages and docs, and builds a voice profile so future drafts sound like them instead of generic AI. The profile is saved as the my-writing-style skill. Use when the user asks to set up, learn, or capture their writing voice, or complains that drafts sound generic or unlike them and no my-writing-style profile exists. Only for drafting text the user will send as themselves, not for Claude's own replies. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["setup-writing-style"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:skill-creator","description":"Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["skill-creator"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:xlsx","description":"Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["xlsx"]}],"uuid":"bca4afeb-693c-442c-bf49-46f304e6f1ed","session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CGiHvfCrN7dtcF9njNxN3u","type":"tool_result","content":"230\t        qid = item.get(\"queue_item_id\")\n231\t        if not isinstance(qid, str) or not qid:\n232\t            continue\n233\t        existing_request = _find_projected_queue_request(\n234\t            base_dir=base_dir, queue_item_id=qid,\n235\t            requests=list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir),\n236\t        )\n237\t        remint_of: str | None = None\n238\t        if existing_request is not None:\n239\t            # Y3 (ORPHAN-703) — a match that died of queue mechanics must\n240\t            # not satisfy idempotency: the shipped check replayed ANY prior\n241\t            # request, so a queue item whose envelope went HUMAN_REQUIRED /\n242\t            # ANCHOR_STALE was consumed forever with no live successor.\n243\t            # Live-or-outcome matches still replay; an eligible dead match\n244\t            # falls through to the mint below with remint_of lineage. The\n245\t            # remint budget mirrors DEFAULT_MAX_REQUEUES: after two\n246\t            # successors the item is disclosed exhausted, not re-minted.\n247\t            from .agent_invocations import derive_request_state\n248\t            from .agent_surface import REMINT_ELIGIBLE_DEAD_STATES\n249\t\n250\t            existing_id = str(existing_request.get(\"request_id\"))\n251\t            state = derive_request_state(request_id=existing_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n252\t            if state not in REMINT_ELIGIBLE_DEAD_STATES:\n253\t                mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n254\t                append_tools_governance(\n255\t                    base_dir,\n256\t                    \"next_cycle_queue_item_projection_replayed\",\n257\t                    {\n258\t                        \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n259\t                        \"request_id\": existing_id,\n260\t                        \"request_state\": state,\n261\t                    },\n262\t                )\n263\t                consumed += 1\n264\t                continue\n265\t            lineage = sum(\n266\t                1 for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir)\n267\t                if row.get(\"remint_of\") and qid in str(row.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"\")\n268\t            )\n269\t            if lineage >= _MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS:\n270\t                mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n271\t                append_tools_governance(\n272\t                    base_dir,\n273\t                    \"next_cycle_queue_item_remint_exhausted\",\n274\t                    {\n275\t                        \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n276\t                        \"request_id\": existing_id,\n277\t                        \"request_state\": state,\n278\t                        \"remint_budget\": _MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS,\n279\t                    },\n280\t                )\n281\t                consumed += 1\n282\t                continue\n283\t            remint_of = existing_id\n284\t        prompt = {\n285\t            \"$schema\": \"aria/next-cycle-queue-request/v1\",\n286\t            \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n287\t            \"source_cycle_id\": item.get(\"source_cycle_id\"),\n288\t            \"pressure_id\": item.get(\"pressure_id\"),\n289\t            \"recommended_action\": item.get(\"recommended_action\"),\n290\t            \"candidate_tools\": item.get(\"candidate_tools\", []),\n291\t        }\n292\t        # Evidence refs come from the pressure's own evidence paths, not from\n293\t        # the pressure identifier. A pressure id (\"pressure:...:repetition\")\n294\t        # cannot parse under the evidence validator's _AGENT_REF_RE, so a\n295\t        # request minted with it as its sole ref was a request no agent could\n296\t        # ever answer with admissible evidence — only an empty-evidence\n297\t        # satisfied verdict could pass, and a blocked/contradicted verdict was\n298\t        # structurally unrepresentable (agent_contract requires refs on\n299\t        # those). Diagnosed by the planner's first accepted response (RC-2).\n300\t        # The identifier keeps its own channel: pressure_event_id below.\n301\t        evidence_refs: list[str] = []\n302\t        source_cycle = str(item.get(\"source_cycle_id\") or \"\")\n303\t        pressure_id = str(item.get(\"pressure_id\") or \"\")\n304\t        # The contract the mint declares. The generic queue projection is the\n305\t        # default; a mission whose `next_action` is a kernel-owned pointer\n306\t        # (`mission_dispatch.NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS`) mints ITS contract\n307\t        # instead — a self_improvement mission asks for evidence_paths /\n308\t        # problem / proposed_change so the accepted answer can reach\n309\t        # `propose_self_change`. Before this, the pointer travelled as free\n310\t        # prompt text and no kernel path ever read it (confirmed live\n311\t        # 2026-09-12; the ORPHAN-694 class).\n312\t        must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]] = [{\n313\t            \"id\": \"queue_item_projected\",\n314\t            \"description\": \"Resolve the queued next-cycle item or produce a concrete blocked reason.\",\n315\t            \"required\": True,\n316\t        }]\n317\t        allowed_scope: list[str] = [\"aria-kernel/**\", \"aria-tools/**\", \".claude/**\"]\n318\t        if pressure_id.startswith(\"mission:\"):\n319\t            # A mission-selection item: the queue key is the mission marker,\n320\t            # and the mission row itself carries the evidence refs its work\n321\t            # accumulated. Resolving here keeps the drain's rule uniform —\n322\t            # refs come from the SOURCE record, never from the identifier.\n323\t            from .mission import fold_mission\n324\t            from .mission_dispatch import contract_for_mission, in_flight_mission_request\n325\t\n326\t            mission_id = pressure_id.split(\":\", 1)[1]\n327\t            try:\n328\t                mission_row = fold_mission(mission_id=mission_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n329\t            except GovernanceError:\n330\t                mission_row = None\n331\t            if mission_row:\n332\t                evidence_refs = [\n333\t                    str(ref) for ref in (mission_row.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n334\t                    if isinstance(ref, str) and ref\n335\t                ]\n336\t                try:\n337\t                    contract = contract_for_mission(\n338\t                        mission_row=mission_row, queue_item_id=qid, source_cycle_id=source_cycle or None,\n339\t                    )\n340\t                except GovernanceError as exc:\n341\t                    append_tools_governance(\n342\t                        base_dir,\n343\t                        \"next_cycle_queue_projection_failed\",\n344\t                        {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"error\": str(exc)},\n345\t                    )\n346\t                    continue\n347\t                if contract is not None:\n348\t                    # A kernel contract is asked once per MISSION, not once\n349\t                    # per queue item: the scheduler re-selects a DISCOVERED\n350\t                    # mission every cycle (nothing moves it until the answer\n351\t                    # lands), and `append_pending` de-duplicates only among\n352\t                    # pending rows, so the second cycle's item is a routine\n353\t                    # duplicate of a question still in flight. The item is\n354\t                    # consumed against the standing request — its answer is\n355\t                    # the answer this item was queued for.\n356\t                    standing = in_flight_mission_request(\n357\t                        mission_id=mission_id, contract=contract, base_dir=base_dir,\n358\t                    )\n359\t                    if standing is not None:\n360\t                        mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n361\t                        append_tools_governance(\n362\t                            base_dir,\n363\t                            \"next_cycle_queue_item_mission_request_in_flight\",\n364\t                            {\n365\t                                \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n366\t                                \"mission_id\": mission_id,\n367\t                                \"request_id\": standing.get(\"request_id\"),\n368\t                                \"request_state\": standing.get(\"request_state\"),\n369\t                                \"contract_ids\": sorted(contract.contract_ids),\n370\t                            },\n371\t                        )\n372\t                        consumed += 1\n373\t                        continue\n374\t                    prompt = contract.prompt\n375\t                    must_satisfy = contract.must_satisfy\n376\t                    allowed_scope = contract.allowed_scope\n377\t        elif source_cycle and pressure_id:\n378\t            try:\n379\t                pressure_record = explain_pressure(\n380\t                    cycle_id=source_cycle, pressure_id=pressure_id, base_dir=base_dir,\n381\t                )\n382\t                evidence_refs = [\n383\t                    str(path) for path in pressure_record.get(\"evidence\") or []\n384\t                    if isinstance(path, str) and path\n385\t                ]\n386\t            except (ValueError, OSError):\n387\t                # No stored pressure payload for that cycle — fall through to\n388\t                # the queue-item marker so the mint still traces to something.\n389\t                evidence_refs = []\n390\t        if not evidence_refs:\n391\t            evidence_refs = [str(qid)]\n392\t        try:\n393\t            request = create_agent_invocation_request(\n394\t                target_agent=\"aria-autonomy-planner\",\n395\t                role=\"maintenance_utility\",\n396\t                suggested_prompt=json.dumps(prompt, indent=2, sort_keys=True),\n397\t                must_satisfy=must_satisfy,\n398\t                allowed_scope=allowed_scope,\n399\t                target_sha=target_sha,\n400\t                evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n401\t                pressure_event_id=pressure_id or None,\n402\t                remint_of=remint_of,\n403\t                base_dir=base_dir,\n404\t            )\n405\t        except Exception as exc:\n406\t            append_tools_governance(\n407\t                base_dir,\n408\t                \"next_cycle_queue_projection_failed\",\n409\t                {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"error\": str(exc)},\n410\t            )\n411\t            continue\n412\t        mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n413\t        append_tools_governance(\n414\t            base_dir,\n415\t            \"next_cycle_queue_item_projected\",\n416\t            {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"request_id\": request.get(\"request_id\")},\n417\t        )\n418\t        consumed += 1\n419\t    return consumed\n420\t\n421\t\n422\t\n423\t# Y3 (ORPHAN-703) — successor budget for dead projected-queue envelopes,\n424\t# mirroring DEFAULT_MAX_REQUEUES: two lineage steps then an exhausted\n425\t# disclosure. Kept beside its only consumer.\n426\t_MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS = 2\n427\t\n428\t\n429\tdef _find_projected_queue_request(\n430\t    *,\n431\t    base_dir: Path,\n432\t    queue_item_id: str,\n433\t    requests: list[dict[str, Any]],\n434\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n435\t    marker = f'\"queue_item_id\": \"{queue_item_id}\"'\n436\t    for request in reversed(requests):\n437\t        if request.get(\"role\") != \"maintenance_utility\":\n438\t            continue\n439\t        if request.get(\"target_agent\") != \"aria-autonomy-planner\":\n440\t            continue\n441\t        prompt = str(request.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"\")\n442\t        if marker in prompt or queue_item_id in prompt:\n443\t            return request\n444\t    return None\n445\t\n446\t# ORPHAN-HIGH-456 — kept next to the summary they bound, so a reviewer sees\n447\t# the cap and the marker list at the point the literal is built.\n448\t_MAX_INCOMPLETE_CYCLES_IN_SUMMARY = 20\n449\t\n450\t# The cycle-level suppression/truncation markers `runtime_artifacts` sums.\n451\t# Mirrored from its `_SUPPRESSED_MARKER_KEYS` / `_TRUNCATED_MARKER_KEYS`; a\n452\t# producer that starts emitting one of these at cycle level is counted\n453\t# without further wiring, which was the stated intent of reading markers\n454\t# rather than incrementing locals.\n455\t_CYCLE_MARKER_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = (\n456\t    \"findings_suppressed\",\n457\t    \"suppressed_count\",\n458\t    \"prompt_truncated\",\n459\t    \"truncated_count\","}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"ac29e877-d77e-4240-b1cb-d788e3930582","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:33:20.241Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/autonomy_orchestrator.py","content":"        qid = item.get(\"queue_item_id\")\n        if not isinstance(qid, str) or not qid:\n            continue\n        existing_request = _find_projected_queue_request(\n            base_dir=base_dir, queue_item_id=qid,\n            requests=list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir),\n        )\n        remint_of: str | None = None\n        if existing_request is not None:\n            # Y3 (ORPHAN-703) — a match that died of queue mechanics must\n            # not satisfy idempotency: the shipped check replayed ANY prior\n            # request, so a queue item whose envelope went HUMAN_REQUIRED /\n            # ANCHOR_STALE was consumed forever with no live successor.\n            # Live-or-outcome matches still replay; an eligible dead match\n            # falls through to the mint below with remint_of lineage. The\n            # remint budget mirrors DEFAULT_MAX_REQUEUES: after two\n            # successors the item is disclosed exhausted, not re-minted.\n            from .agent_invocations import derive_request_state\n            from .agent_surface import REMINT_ELIGIBLE_DEAD_STATES\n\n            existing_id = str(existing_request.get(\"request_id\"))\n            state = derive_request_state(request_id=existing_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n            if state not in REMINT_ELIGIBLE_DEAD_STATES:\n                mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n                append_tools_governance(\n                    base_dir,\n                    \"next_cycle_queue_item_projection_replayed\",\n                    {\n                        \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n                        \"request_id\": existing_id,\n                        \"request_state\": state,\n                    },\n                )\n                consumed += 1\n                continue\n            lineage = sum(\n                1 for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir)\n                if row.get(\"remint_of\") and qid in str(row.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"\")\n            )\n            if lineage >= _MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS:\n                mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n                append_tools_governance(\n                    base_dir,\n                    \"next_cycle_queue_item_remint_exhausted\",\n                    {\n                        \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n                        \"request_id\": existing_id,\n                        \"request_state\": state,\n                        \"remint_budget\": _MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS,\n                    },\n                )\n                consumed += 1\n                continue\n            remint_of = existing_id\n        prompt = {\n            \"$schema\": \"aria/next-cycle-queue-request/v1\",\n            \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n            \"source_cycle_id\": item.get(\"source_cycle_id\"),\n            \"pressure_id\": item.get(\"pressure_id\"),\n            \"recommended_action\": item.get(\"recommended_action\"),\n            \"candidate_tools\": item.get(\"candidate_tools\", []),\n        }\n        # Evidence refs come from the pressure's own evidence paths, not from\n        # the pressure identifier. A pressure id (\"pressure:...:repetition\")\n        # cannot parse under the evidence validator's _AGENT_REF_RE, so a\n        # request minted with it as its sole ref was a request no agent could\n        # ever answer with admissible evidence — only an empty-evidence\n        # satisfied verdict could pass, and a blocked/contradicted verdict was\n        # structurally unrepresentable (agent_contract requires refs on\n        # those). Diagnosed by the planner's first accepted response (RC-2).\n        # The identifier keeps its own channel: pressure_event_id below.\n        evidence_refs: list[str] = []\n        source_cycle = str(item.get(\"source_cycle_id\") or \"\")\n        pressure_id = str(item.get(\"pressure_id\") or \"\")\n        # The contract the mint declares. The generic queue projection is the\n        # default; a mission whose `next_action` is a kernel-owned pointer\n        # (`mission_dispatch.NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS`) mints ITS contract\n        # instead — a self_improvement mission asks for evidence_paths /\n        # problem / proposed_change so the accepted answer can reach\n        # `propose_self_change`. Before this, the pointer travelled as free\n        # prompt text and no kernel path ever read it (confirmed live\n        # 2026-09-12; the ORPHAN-694 class).\n        must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]] = [{\n            \"id\": \"queue_item_projected\",\n            \"description\": \"Resolve the queued next-cycle item or produce a concrete blocked reason.\",\n            \"required\": True,\n        }]\n        allowed_scope: list[str] = [\"aria-kernel/**\", \"aria-tools/**\", \".claude/**\"]\n        if pressure_id.startswith(\"mission:\"):\n            # A mission-selection item: the queue key is the mission marker,\n            # and the mission row itself carries the evidence refs its work\n            # accumulated. Resolving here keeps the drain's rule uniform —\n            # refs come from the SOURCE record, never from the identifier.\n            from .mission import fold_mission\n            from .mission_dispatch import contract_for_mission, in_flight_mission_request\n\n            mission_id = pressure_id.split(\":\", 1)[1]\n            try:\n                mission_row = fold_mission(mission_id=mission_id, base_dir=base_dir)\n            except GovernanceError:\n                mission_row = None\n            if mission_row:\n                evidence_refs = [\n                    str(ref) for ref in (mission_row.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n                    if isinstance(ref, str) and ref\n                ]\n                try:\n                    contract = contract_for_mission(\n                        mission_row=mission_row, queue_item_id=qid, source_cycle_id=source_cycle or None,\n                    )\n                except GovernanceError as exc:\n                    append_tools_governance(\n                        base_dir,\n                        \"next_cycle_queue_projection_failed\",\n                        {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"error\": str(exc)},\n                    )\n                    continue\n                if contract is not None:\n                    # A kernel contract is asked once per MISSION, not once\n                    # per queue item: the scheduler re-selects a DISCOVERED\n                    # mission every cycle (nothing moves it until the answer\n                    # lands), and `append_pending` de-duplicates only among\n                    # pending rows, so the second cycle's item is a routine\n                    # duplicate of a question still in flight. The item is\n                    # consumed against the standing request — its answer is\n                    # the answer this item was queued for.\n                    standing = in_flight_mission_request(\n                        mission_id=mission_id, contract=contract, base_dir=base_dir,\n                    )\n                    if standing is not None:\n                        mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n                        append_tools_governance(\n                            base_dir,\n                            \"next_cycle_queue_item_mission_request_in_flight\",\n                            {\n                                \"queue_item_id\": qid,\n                                \"mission_id\": mission_id,\n                                \"request_id\": standing.get(\"request_id\"),\n                                \"request_state\": standing.get(\"request_state\"),\n                                \"contract_ids\": sorted(contract.contract_ids),\n                            },\n                        )\n                        consumed += 1\n                        continue\n                    prompt = contract.prompt\n                    must_satisfy = contract.must_satisfy\n                    allowed_scope = contract.allowed_scope\n        elif source_cycle and pressure_id:\n            try:\n                pressure_record = explain_pressure(\n                    cycle_id=source_cycle, pressure_id=pressure_id, base_dir=base_dir,\n                )\n                evidence_refs = [\n                    str(path) for path in pressure_record.get(\"evidence\") or []\n                    if isinstance(path, str) and path\n                ]\n            except (ValueError, OSError):\n                # No stored pressure payload for that cycle — fall through to\n                # the queue-item marker so the mint still traces to something.\n                evidence_refs = []\n        if not evidence_refs:\n            evidence_refs = [str(qid)]\n        try:\n            request = create_agent_invocation_request(\n                target_agent=\"aria-autonomy-planner\",\n                role=\"maintenance_utility\",\n                suggested_prompt=json.dumps(prompt, indent=2, sort_keys=True),\n                must_satisfy=must_satisfy,\n                allowed_scope=allowed_scope,\n                target_sha=target_sha,\n                evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n                pressure_event_id=pressure_id or None,\n                remint_of=remint_of,\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n        except Exception as exc:\n            append_tools_governance(\n                base_dir,\n                \"next_cycle_queue_projection_failed\",\n                {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"error\": str(exc)},\n            )\n            continue\n        mark_consumed(base_dir, queue_item_id=qid, consumed_by=daemon_agent_id)\n        append_tools_governance(\n            base_dir,\n            \"next_cycle_queue_item_projected\",\n            {\"queue_item_id\": qid, \"request_id\": request.get(\"request_id\")},\n        )\n        consumed += 1\n    return consumed\n\n\n\n# Y3 (ORPHAN-703) — successor budget for dead projected-queue envelopes,\n# mirroring DEFAULT_MAX_REQUEUES: two lineage steps then an exhausted\n# disclosure. Kept beside its only consumer.\n_MAX_QUEUE_ITEM_REMINTS = 2\n\n\ndef _find_projected_queue_request(\n    *,\n    base_dir: Path,\n    queue_item_id: str,\n    requests: list[dict[str, Any]],\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    marker = f'\"queue_item_id\": \"{queue_item_id}\"'\n    for request in reversed(requests):\n        if request.get(\"role\") != \"maintenance_utility\":\n            continue\n        if request.get(\"target_agent\") != \"aria-autonomy-planner\":\n            continue\n        prompt = str(request.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"\")\n        if marker in prompt or queue_item_id in prompt:\n            return request\n    return None\n\n# ORPHAN-HIGH-456 — kept next to the summary they bound, so a reviewer sees\n# the cap and the marker list at the point the literal is built.\n_MAX_INCOMPLETE_CYCLES_IN_SUMMARY = 20\n\n# The cycle-level suppression/truncation markers `runtime_artifacts` sums.\n# Mirrored from its `_SUPPRESSED_MARKER_KEYS` / `_TRUNCATED_MARKER_KEYS`; a\n# producer that starts emitting one of these at cycle level is counted\n# without further wiring, which was the stated intent of reading markers\n# rather than incrementing locals.\n_CYCLE_MARKER_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"findings_suppressed\",\n    \"suppressed_count\",\n    \"prompt_truncated\",\n    \"truncated_count\",","numLines":230,"startLine":230,"totalLines":2833}}}

Worst-case time stays linear in input length even for\n35\t# pathological inputs that the pre-V8.3 ReDoS pattern hung on.\n36\t#\n37\t# Plan ARIA-V8.6 — accept the third `path:line:content` form. The\n38\t# `plan_synthesizer` (V7.9) emits evidence_refs as\n39\t# `path:line:<excerpt>` triplets so operators can spot-check the\n40\t# claimed line text without opening every file. The pre-V8.6 regex\n41\t# rejected those refs as malformed because it only allowed the\n42\t# 2-part `path:line` form, so the agent's response evidence_refs\n43\t# (which echo the request's refs) hit `agent_evidence_ref_malformed`\n44\t# at the kernel validator. Three\n51\t# grades reach this mapping and only one of them is about the evidence:\n52\t#\n53\t# * ``worktree_candidate`` — the tree was read and disagrees: the agent's.\n54\t# * ``baseline_unavailable`` — the caller threaded no target_sha, so no\n55\t#   comparison was attempted (2026-08-09): the harness's.\n56\t# * ``verification_unavailable`` — a comparison was attempted and git did not\n57\t#   answer inside its bound (2026-09-12, loaded host): the harness's.\n58\t#\n59\t# One table for both evidence sources so the tool-output side and the agent\n60\t# side cannot drift apart again — the agent side kept a single code for a\n61\t# month after the tool side had split.\n62\t_UNVERIFIED_GRADE_CODE_SUFFIX: dict[str, str] = {\n63\t    \"baseline_unavailable\": \"evidence_baseline_unavailable\",\n64\t    \"verification_unavailable\": \"evidence_verification_unavailable\",\n65\t}\n66\t_UNVERIFIED_DEFAULT_CODE_SUFFIX = \"evidence_not_repo_verified\"\n67\t\n68\t# The rejection codes that mean \"the validator could not verify\", on either\n69\t# source. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MxE5CG9jjbNWnYRGK5JFmi","type":"tool_result","content":"440\t            resolved = (\n441\t                row_path.resolve()\n442\t                if row_path.is_absolute()\n443\t                else (ledger.parent.parent / output_path).resolve()\n444\t            )\n445\t        except OSError:\n446\t            continue\n447\t        if resolved == candidate:\n448\t            recorded = output_hash\n449\t            break\n450\t    if recorded is None:\n451\t        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n452\t    if not candidate.exists() or not candidate.is_file():\n453\t        return False, \"artifact_missing\"\n454\t    digest = \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n455\t    if digest != recorded:\n456\t        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n457\t    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n458\t\n459\t\n460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})\n520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)\n530\t    if line is None:\n531\t        return\n532\t    if line <= 0:\n533\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n534\t        return\n535\t    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n536\t    if line > line_count:\n537\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n538\t\n539\t\n540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"3f001e1f-35f1-4a83-b5e0-a7165b5aa563","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:33:23.009Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"            resolved = (\n                row_path.resolve()\n                if row_path.is_absolute()\n                else (ledger.parent.parent / output_path).resolve()\n            )\n        except OSError:\n            continue\n        if resolved == candidate:\n            recorded = output_hash\n            break\n    if recorded is None:\n        return False, \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\"\n    if not candidate.exists() or not candidate.is_file():\n        return False, \"artifact_missing\"\n    digest = \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n    if digest != recorded:\n        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n\n\ndef _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})\n        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)\n    if line is None:\n        return\n    if line <= 0:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n        return\n    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n    if line > line_count:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n\n\ndef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],","numLines":120,"startLine":440,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0117WfqfYtxZqK1sk6UtRj2X","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The self-change contract: what a `propose_self_change` mission asks an agent, and how the answer reaches the kernel.\n2\t\n3\tWHY: `open_self_improvement_missions` (Plan 032 Faz 032i) minted missions\n4\twhose `next_action == \"propose_self_change\"`, the orchestrator's mission\n5\tbranch forwarded that string as free prompt text under the generic\n6\t`queue_item_projected` contract, and NOTHING mapped an accepted answer onto\n7\t`propose_self_change` — so `authority_surface_violations` and the\n8\tHUMAN_REQUIRED adjudication could only ever fire from the CLI. Confirmed\n9\tlive 2026-09-12 (the ORPHAN-694 class: mechanism present, caller absent).\n10\t\n11\tWHAT: one contract, three consumers.\n12\t* `self_change_must_satisfy()` / `build_self_change_prompt()` — what the\n13\t  mission branch mints (`aria/self-change-request/v1`): the agent must answer\n14\t  with `details.evidence_paths`, `details.problem`, `details.proposed_change`.\n15\t* `validate_self_change_response()` — the SHAPE check, used by the executor's\n16\t  pre-submit gate (release + retry, harness-class) and by the bridge, so the\n17\t  two can never disagree about what a well-formed answer is (the Y5 lesson).\n18\t* `record_self_change_result()` — the accepted-result bridge: reads the\n19\t  structured fields off the envelope, calls `propose_self_change` (which is\n20\t  where the AUTHORITY boundary is judged — authority surfaces AND the\n21\t  mission's state, pointer and open adjudication — and the HUMAN_REQUIRED\n22\t  opens), then parks the mission in HUMAN_REQUIRED, a WAITING state\n23\t  `mission_scheduler.select_next_mission` skips. A refusal raises before the\n24\t  park, so a refused answer leaves the mission exactly as it was.\n25\t\n26\tThe bridge dispatches on the CONTRACT the request declared (its must_satisfy\n27\tids, part of the request identity), never on the agent's prose; the mission\n28\tidentity comes from the request's `pressure_event_id` (D1: identity from what\n29\twas asked, never from what the agent volunteers).\n30\t\"\"\"\n31\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n32\t\n33\tfrom pathlib import Path\n34\tfrom typing import Any\n35\t\n36\tfrom .self_improvement import (\n37\t    AUTHORITY_SURFACES,\n38\t    DEFAULT_VALIDATION_COMMAND,\n39\t    SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES,\n40\t    SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION,\n41\t)\n42\tfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError, ensure_tools_dir\n43\t\n44\tSELF_CHANGE_REQUEST_SCHEMA = \"aria/self-change-request/v1\"\n45\t# The same lane the queue projection uses: the planner is read-only and\n46\t# kernel-envelope only, which is exactly the shape a PROPOSAL needs — the\n47\t# change itself is applied by nobody until a person adjudicates.\n48\tSELF_CHANGE_TARGET_AGENT = \"aria-autonomy-planner\"\n49\tSELF_CHANGE_ROLE = \"maintenance_utility\"\n50\t# The structured fields `propose_self_change` takes, in the order the\n51\t# contract names them; `details.<field>` on the response envelope.\n52\tSELF_CHANGE_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (\"evidence_paths\", \"problem\", \"proposed_change\")\n53\tSELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY: tuple[dict[str, str], ...] = (\n54\t    {\"id\": \"self_change_evidence_paths\",\n55\t     \"description\": \"details.evidence_paths is a non-empty list of repo-relative file paths that evidence the defect, \"\n56\t                  \"every one inside ARIA's own scope (allowed_prefixes) and none on an authority surface \"\n57\t                  \"(authority_surfaces); each path was read at the request's target SHA\"},\n58\t    {\"id\": \"self_change_problem\",\n59\t     \"description\": \"details.problem states the defect the mission's signal evidences, grounded in evidence_paths\"},\n60\t    {\"id\": \"self_change_proposed_change\",\n61\t     \"description\": \"details.proposed_change states the concrete change to ARIA's own code and how validation_command proves it\"},\n62\t)\n63\tSELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_IDS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(item[\"id\"] for item in SELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY)\n64\t# The executor's release reason for a malformed answer — harness-class (the\n65\t# shape says nothing about the mission; re-asking usually succeeds), listed\n66\t# in agent_invocations.HARNESS_FAULT_RELEASE_REASONS.\n67\tSELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_RELEASE_REASON = \"self_change_contract_violation\"\n68\tSELF_CHANGE_STEP_PREFIX = \"self_change:\"\n69\tSELF_CHANGE_MISSION_REASON = \"self_change_adjudication\"\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef self_change_must_satisfy() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n73\t    return [dict(item) for item in SELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY]\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tdef self_change_allowed_scope() -> list[str]:\n77\t    \"\"\"`SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES` as evidence-validator globs (`dir/**`, `stem*`).\"\"\"\n78\t    return [prefix + \"**\" if prefix.endswith(\"/\") else prefix + \"*\" for prefix in SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES]\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef is_self_change_request(request: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n82\t    \"\"\"A request whose declared contract IS the self-change contract.\"\"\"\n83\t    ids = {str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in (request.get(\"must_satisfy\") or []) if isinstance(item, dict)}\n84\t    return ids == set(SELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_IDS)\n85\t\n86\t\n87\tdef mission_id_of(request: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:\n88\t    marker = str(request.get(\"pressure_event_id\") or \"\")\n89\t    return marker.split(\":\", 1)[1] if marker.startswith(\"mission:\") else None\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef build_self_change_prompt(*, queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None, mission_row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n93\t    \"\"\"The sealed prompt: the mission, the boundary, and the answer shape — no free text.\"\"\"\n94\t    return {\n95\t        \"$schema\": SELF_CHANGE_REQUEST_SCHEMA,\n96\t        \"queue_item_id\": queue_item_id,\n97\t        \"source_cycle_id\": source_cycle_id,\n98\t        \"mission_id\": str(mission_row.get(\"mission_id\") or \"\"),\n99\t        \"source_id\": mission_row.get(\"source_id\"),\n100\t        \"title\": mission_row.get(\"title\"),\n101\t        \"next_action\": SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION,\n102\t        \"response_details_fields\": list(SELF_CHANGE_FIELDS),\n103\t        \"allowed_prefixes\": list(SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES),\n104\t        \"authority_surfaces\": list(AUTHORITY_SURFACES),\n105\t        \"validation_command\": self-change contract: what a `propose_self_change` mission asks an agent, and how the answer reaches the kernel.\n\nWHY: `open_self_improvement_missions` (Plan 032 Faz 032i) minted missions\nwhose `next_action == \"propose_self_change\"`, the orchestrator's mission\nbranch forwarded that string as free prompt text under the generic\n`queue_item_projected` contract, and NOTHING mapped an accepted answer onto\n`propose_self_change` — so `authority_surface_violations` and the\nHUMAN_REQUIRED adjudication could only ever fire from the CLI. Confirmed\nlive 2026-09-12 (the ORPHAN-694 class: mechanism present, caller absent).\n\nWHAT: one contract, three consumers.\n* `self_change_must_satisfy()` / `build_self_change_prompt()` — what the\n  mission branch mints (`aria/self-change-request/v1`): the agent must answer\n  with `details.evidence_paths`, `details.problem`, `details.proposed_change`.\n* `validate_self_change_response()` — the SHAPE check, used by the executor's\n  pre-submit gate (release + retry, harness-class) and by the bridge, so the\n  two can never disagree about what a well-formed answer is (the Y5 lesson).\n* `record_self_change_result()` — the accepted-result bridge: reads the\n  structured fields off the envelope, calls `propose_self_change` (which is\n  where the AUTHORITY boundary is judged — authority surfaces AND the\n  mission's state, pointer and open adjudication — and the HUMAN_REQUIRED\n  opens), then parks the mission in HUMAN_REQUIRED, a WAITING state\n  `mission_scheduler.select_next_mission` skips. A refusal raises before the\n  park, so a refused answer leaves the mission exactly as it was.\n\nThe bridge dispatches on the CONTRACT the request declared (its must_satisfy\nids, part of the request identity), never on the agent's prose; the mission\nidentity comes from the request's `pressure_event_id` (D1: identity from what\nwas asked, never from what the agent volunteers).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom .self_improvement import (\n    AUTHORITY_SURFACES,\n    DEFAULT_VALIDATION_COMMAND,\n    SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES,\n    SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION,\n)\nfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError, ensure_tools_dir\n\nSELF_CHANGE_REQUEST_SCHEMA = \"aria/self-change-request/v1\"\n# The same lane the queue projection uses: the planner is read-only and\n# kernel-envelope only, which is exactly the shape a PROPOSAL needs — the\n# change itself is applied by nobody until a person adjudicates.\nSELF_CHANGE_TARGET_AGENT = \"aria-autonomy-planner\"\nSELF_CHANGE_ROLE = \"maintenance_utility\"\n# The structured fields `propose_self_change` takes, in the order the\n# contract names them; `details.<field>` on the response envelope.\nSELF_CHANGE_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (\"evidence_paths\", \"problem\", \"proposed_change\")\nSELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY: tuple[dict[str, str], ...] = (\n    {\"id\": \"self_change_evidence_paths\",\n     \"description\": \"details.evidence_paths is a non-empty list of repo-relative file paths that evidence the defect, \"\n                  \"every one inside ARIA's own scope (allowed_prefixes) and none on an authority surface \"\n                  \"(authority_surfaces); each path was read at the request's target SHA\"},\n    {\"id\": \"self_change_problem\",\n     \"description\": \"details.problem states the defect the mission's signal evidences, grounded in evidence_paths\"},\n    {\"id\": \"self_change_proposed_change\",\n     \"description\": \"details.proposed_change states the concrete change to ARIA's own code and how validation_command proves it\"},\n)\nSELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_IDS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(item[\"id\"] for item in SELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY)\n# The executor's release reason for a malformed answer — harness-class (the\n# shape says nothing about the mission; re-asking usually succeeds), listed\n# in agent_invocations.HARNESS_FAULT_RELEASE_REASONS.\nSELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_RELEASE_REASON = \"self_change_contract_violation\"\nSELF_CHANGE_STEP_PREFIX = \"self_change:\"\nSELF_CHANGE_MISSION_REASON = \"self_change_adjudication\"\n\n\ndef self_change_must_satisfy() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    return [dict(item) for item in SELF_CHANGE_MUST_SATISFY]\n\n\ndef self_change_allowed_scope() -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"`SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES` as evidence-validator globs (`dir/**`, `stem*`).\"\"\"\n    return [prefix + \"**\" if prefix.endswith(\"/\") else prefix + \"*\" for prefix in SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES]\n\n\ndef is_self_change_request(request: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"A request whose declared contract IS the self-change contract.\"\"\"\n    ids = {str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in (request.get(\"must_satisfy\") or []) if isinstance(item, dict)}\n    return ids == set(SELF_CHANGE_CONTRACT_IDS)\n\n\ndef mission_id_of(request: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:\n    marker = str(request.get(\"pressure_event_id\") or \"\")\n    return marker.split(\":\", 1)[1] if marker.startswith(\"mission:\") else None\n\n\ndef build_self_change_prompt(*, queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None, mission_row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"The sealed prompt: the mission, the boundary, and the answer shape — no free text.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"$schema\": SELF_CHANGE_REQUEST_SCHEMA,\n        \"queue_item_id\": queue_item_id,\n        \"source_cycle_id\": source_cycle_id,\n        \"mission_id\": str(mission_row.get(\"mission_id\") or \"\"),\n        \"source_id\": mission_row.get(\"source_id\"),\n        \"title\": mission_row.get(\"title\"),\n        \"next_action\": SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION,\n        \"response_details_fields\": list(SELF_CHANGE_FIELDS),\n        \"allowed_prefixes\": list(SELF_CHANGE_ALLOWED_PREFIXES),\n        \"authority_surfaces\": list(AUTHORITY_SURFACES),\n        \"validation_command\": DEFAULT_VALIDATION_COMMAND,\n    }\n\n\ndef validate_self_change_response(*, request: dict[str, Any], response: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Shape errors of a self-change answer, named per contract id; [] for other requests.\n\n    Shape only — the authority boundary is judged by `propose_self_change`\n    in the kernel, so a refusal is a governance row and not a silent\n    pre-submit release.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not is_self_change_request(request):\n        return []\n    details = response.get(\"details\")\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":197}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013mWgH8e3TdnACBHgp3hMv4","type":"tool_result","content":"363\tdef validate_response(\n364\t    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n365\t    *,\n366\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n367\t    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n368\t) -> None:\n369\t    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n370\t\n371\t    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n372\t    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n373\t\n374\t    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n375\t    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n376\t    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n377\t    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n378\t    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n379\t    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n380\t    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n381\t    claim/agent) would pass.\n382\t    \"\"\"\n383\t    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n384\t    if missing:\n385\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n386\t    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n387\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n388\t    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n389\t validate_response(\n    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n\n    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n\n    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n    claim/agent) would pass.\n    \"\"\"\n    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n    if missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n\n    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n            )\n        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n                )\n            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n            _ensure_string_list(\n                evidence_refs,\n                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n                allow_empty=False,\n            )\n        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n\n    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n\n    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n    # agent_id differs. `role` must equal the request's `role`.\",\n        f\"- `satisfaction_matrix[]`: exactly one entry per `must_satisfy[].id` (no extra ids, no\",\n        f\"  duplicates), each `{{id, verdict, note?, evidence_refs?}}` with `verdict` ∈ {{{verdicts}}}.\",\n        \"- `verdict` = `blocked` or `contradicted` REQUIRES a non-empty `note` (the reason, prose) AND\",\n        \"  a non-empty `evidence_refs` list. `evidence` is not read; field=\"refusal.evidence_refs\", allow_empty=True\n    )\n    payload = {\n        \"$schema\": REFUSAL_SCHEMA,\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"cycle_id\": cycle_id,\n        \"refused_by\": refused_by,\n        \"reason_class\": reason_class,\n        \"reason_text\": reason_text,\n        \"evidence_refs\": refs,\n    }\n    if at is not None:\n        payload[\"at\"] = at\n    return payload\n\n\ndef envelope_hash(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Stable SHA-256 of a request or response envelope.\n\n    Used by callers needing a content-addressed identifier independent of\n    the queue ledger's hash chain (e.g. cross-checking a submitted output's\n    `output_hash` against the request fingerprint).\n    \"\"\"\n    canonical = json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), ensure_ascii=True)\n    return \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n","numLines":254,"startLine":363,"totalLines":616}}}
