{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfCtEcvTFxH9ztEG1RuVh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015FENyCrVdaTh1psAXZfEwo","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","offset":540,"limit":200},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5468,"cache_read_input_tokens":100920,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5468},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"5a8b2171-d0ca-48bf-b3f1-fba03ad25e37","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:34:22.780Z","request_id":"req_011CfCtEcKV8c5UPxyg38UJB","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_015FENyCrVdaTh1psAXZfEwo":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","offset":540,"limit":200}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015FENyCrVdaTh1psAXZfEwo","type":"tool_result","content":"540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n560\t        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n561\t    }\n562\t    \"\"\"\n563\t    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n564\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n565\t    checked: list[str] = []\n566\t    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n567\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n568\t    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n569\t    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n570\t    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n571\t        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n572\t    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n573\t    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n574\t    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n575\t    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n576\t    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n577\t    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n578\t    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n579\t    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n580\t    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n581\t    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n582\t\n583\t    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n584\t    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n585\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n586\t        response_refs = []\n587\t    for ref in response_refs:\n588\t        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n589\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n590\t            continue\n591\t        _check_agent_ref(\n592\t            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n593\t            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n594\t        )\n595\t        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n596\t            continue  # Z2 — a ledger pointer has no repo classification\n597\t        if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n598\t            # Verified by content hash against the results ledger — the\n599\t            # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n600\t            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n601\t            continue\n602\t        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n603\t            ref,\n604\t            workspace_root=root,\n605\t            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n606\t            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n607\t            probe_session=probe_session,\n608\t        )\n609\t        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n610\t        try:\n611\t            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n612\t        except GovernanceError as exc:\n613\t            errors.append({\n614\t                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n615\t                \"ref\": ref,\n616\t                \"reason\": str(exc),\n617\t            })\n618\t\n619\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n620\t    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n621\t    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n622\t    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n623\t    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n624\t    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n625\t    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n626\t    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n627\t    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n628\t    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n629\t    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n630\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n631\t    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n632\t        for entry in matrix:\n633\t            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n634\t                continue\n635\t            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n636\t            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n637\t                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n638\t                continue\n639\t            for ref in entry_refs:\n640\t                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n641\t                    errors.append(\n642\t                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n643\t                    )\n644\t                    continue\n645\t                _check_agent_ref(\n646\t                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n647\t                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n648\t                )\n649\t                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n650\t                    continue  # Refs\n673\t    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n674\t    #\n675\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n676\t    # supplied. Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n677\t    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n678\t    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n679\t    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n680\t    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n681\t    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n682\t    # present.\n683\t    if request is not None:\n684\t        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n685\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n686\t        else:\n687\t            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n688\t            allowed_request_refs = {\n689\t                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n690\t                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n691\t                if isinstance(r, str)\n692\t            }\n693\t            for path in checked:\n694\t                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n695\t                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n696\t                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n697\t                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n698\t                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n699\t                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n700\t                    continue\n701\t                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n702\t                    continue\n703\t                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n704\t                    errors.append(\n705\t                        {\n706\t                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n707\t                            \"path\": path,\n708\t                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n709\t                        }\n710\t                    )\n711\t\n712\t    return {\n713\t        \"valid\": not errors,\n714\t        \"errors\": errors,\n715\t        \"checked_refs\": sorted(set(checked)),\n716\t        \"evidence_envelopes\": evidence_envelopes,\n717\t    }\n718\t\n719\t\n720\tdef _request_target_sha(request: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n721\t    if not request:\n722\t        return None\n723\t    # ARIA-HIGH-022 — an executor may ground the evidence check at the AGENT's\n724\t    # committed HEAD (`evidence_target_sha`) instead of the request's base:\n725\t    # implementer agents cite the POST-FIX lines of files they changed, which\n726\t    # can never match the pre-edit blob, so every genuine fix graded\n727\t    # worktree_candidate and the submit was rejected. The override is only\n728\t    # honoured when submit_claim_result PROVED it descends from the request's\n729\t    # base (fail-closed there); a commit that contains the base's tree plus\n730\t    # the agent's proven work is a strictly stronger anchor than the base.\n731\t    override = request.get(\"evidence_target_sha\")\n732\t    if isinstance(override, str) and override.strip():\n733\t        return override.strip()\n734\t    value = request.get(\"target_sha\") or request.get(\"base_commit_sha\") or request.get(\"pinned_commit_sha\")\n735\t    return str(value).strip() if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() else None\n736\t\n737\t\n738\tdef _snapshot_target_sha(snapshot: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n739\t    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"69bc0cab-67a4-47eb-9359-40ac6eec5139","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:34:23.153Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"def validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    checked: list[str] = []\n    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n\n    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n        response_refs = []\n    for ref in response_refs:\n        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n            continue\n        _check_agent_ref(\n            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n        )\n        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n            continue  # Z2 — a ledger pointer has no repo classification\n        if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n            # Verified by content hash against the results ledger — the\n            # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n            continue\n        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n            ref,\n            workspace_root=root,\n            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n            probe_session=probe_session,\n        )\n        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n        try:\n            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n        except GovernanceError as exc:\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": str(exc),\n            })\n\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n        for entry in matrix:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n                continue\n            for ref in entry_refs:\n                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _check_agent_ref(\n                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n                )\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n                    continue  # Z2 — same single definition as above\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n                    continue  # ORPHAN-734 — same single definition as above\n                envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n                    ref,\n                    workspace_root=root,\n                    source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n                    target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n                    probe_session=probe_session,\n                )\n                evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n                try:\n                    EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n                except GovernanceError as exc:\n                    errors.append({\n                        \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                        \"ref\": ref,\n                        \"reason\": str(exc),\n                    })\n\n    # Cross-check: when a request is provided, every ref the agent\n    # claims must either live inside `allowed_scope` OR be one of the\n    # request's evidence_refs (the canonical bounding box). Refs\n    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n    #\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n    # supplied. Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n    # present.\n    if request is not None:\n        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n        else:\n            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n            allowed_request_refs = {\n                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n                if isinstance(r, str)\n            }\n            for path in checked:\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n                    continue\n                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n                    continue\n                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\n                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n                            \"path\": path,\n                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n                        }\n                    )\n\n    return {\n        \"valid\": not errors,\n        \"errors\": errors,\n        \"checked_refs\": sorted(set(checked)),\n        \"evidence_envelopes\": evidence_envelopes,\n    }\n\n\ndef _request_target_sha(request: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n    if not request:\n        return None\n    # ARIA-HIGH-022 — an executor may ground the evidence check at the AGENT's\n    # committed HEAD (`evidence_target_sha`) instead of the request's base:\n    # implementer agents cite the POST-FIX lines of files they changed, which\n    # can never match the pre-edit blob, so every genuine fix graded\n    # worktree_candidate and the submit was rejected. The override is only\n    # honoured when submit_claim_result PROVED it descends from the request's\n    # base (fail-closed there); a commit that contains the base's tree plus\n    # the agent's proven work is a strictly stronger anchor than the base.\n    override = request.get(\"evidence_target_sha\")\n    if isinstance(override, str) and override.strip():\n        return override.strip()\n    value = request.get(\"target_sha\") or request.get(\"base_commit_sha\") or request.get(\"pinned_commit_sha\")\n    return str(value).strip() if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() else None\n\n\ndef _snapshot_target_sha(snapshot: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):","numLines":200,"startLine":540,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012xM5CwTrntHB6KZjeP8ePn","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Which agent contract a mission's `next_action` pointer mints — the table the queue drain reads.\n2\t\n3\tWHY: a mission's forward pointer (`next_action`, ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730) was\n4\tforwarded by the orchestrator's mission branch as FREE PROMPT TEXT\n5\t(`recommended_action`) under one generic contract, whatever it said. Two\n6\tkernel-owned pointers existed and neither was read by anyone:\n7\t`SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION` (a self-change must reach `propose_self_change`\n8\twith structured fields) and `ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION` (a labelled GitHub\n9\tissue). Confirmed live 2026-09-12.\n10\t\n11\tWHAT: two tables that PARTITION the kernel-minted pointers, the same shape\n12\tas `gateway.default_schedules` (`DEFAULT_SCHEDULES` / `OPERATOR_ONLY_ACTIONS`):\n13\t* `NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS` — pointers whose answer has a KERNEL consumer.\n14\t  The builder returns the prompt, must_satisfy and allowed_scope the drain\n15\t  mints; the accepted answer is bridged to that consumer.\n16\t* `GENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS` — pointers for which the generic queue\n17\t  projection IS the dispatch, each with the mandatory reason and the NAMED\n18\t  consumer that reads the mission by its `source_kind`. A bare ``None`` in\n19\t  the contract table used to say the same thing with no reason and no\n20\t  consumer — an enumeration escape hatch a new pointer could hide in.\n21\tA pointer in neither table, or in both, fails\n22\t`tests/test_self_change_bridge.py::EveryNextActionHasADispatcher`, so a new\n23\t`*_NEXT_ACTION` constant cannot ship minted-but-unread.\n24\t\n25\tA contract is asked ONCE PER MISSION while a request is in flight\n26\t(`in_flight_mission_request`): the scheduler never moves a DISCOVERED\n27\tmission and the queue de-duplicates per pending item, so without this rule\n28\tevery cycle re-queued the same mission and the drain minted a second\n29\trequest for a question the first had not yet answered.\n30\t\"\"\"\n31\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n32\t\n33\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n34\tfrom pathlib import Path\n35\tfrom typing import Any, Callable\n36\t\n37\tfrom .gateway.router import ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION, ISSUE_MISSION_SOURCE_KIND\n38\tfrom .self_change_bridge import build_self_change_prompt, self_change_allowed_scope, self_change_must_satisfy\n39\tfrom .self_improvement import SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION, is_self_change_mission\n40\tfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n41\t\n42\t\n43\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n44\tclass MissionContract:\n45\t    prompt: dict[str, Any]\n46\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]]\n47\t    allowed_scope: list[str]\n48\t\n49\t    @property\n50\t    def contract_ids(self) -> frozenset[str]:\n51\t        \"\"\"The contract's identity: what `is_self_change_request` and the in-flight lookup compare.\"\"\"\n52\t        return frozenset(str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in self.must_satisfy if isinstance(item, dict))\n53\t\n54\t\n55\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n56\tclass GenericProjection:\n57\t    \"\"\"A pointer the generic queue projection dispatches — with the proof that someone reads the mission.\n58\t\n59\t    ``consumer`` is ``\"<module>:<function>\"``: a scanner that takes\n60\t    ``workspace_root`` and returns candidate rows whose ``candidate_id`` is\n61\t    the mission id, for missions of ``source_kind``. The invariant test\n62\t    resolves it and runs it against a minted mission, so the entry cannot\n63\t    name a consumer that does not exist or does not read this source kind.\n64\t    \"\"\"\n65\t    source_kind: str\n66\t    consumer: str\n67\t    reason: str\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tContractBuilder = Callable[..., MissionContract]\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef _self_change_contract(*, queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None, mission_row: dict[str, Any]) -> MissionContract:\n74\t    if not is_self_change_mission(mission_row):\n75\t        # The pointer alone is not the discriminator: a foreign source kind\n76\t        # carrying it is a producer defect, disclosed at the mint rather\n77\t        # than silently handed the generic contract.\n78\t        raise GovernanceError(\n79\t            f\"self_change_pointer_on_foreign_mission:{mission_row.get('mission_id')}:{mission_row.get('source_kind')}\"\n80\t        )\n81\t    return MissionContract(\n82\t        prompt=build_self_change_prompt(queue_item_id=queue_item_id, source_cycle_id=source_cycle_id, mission_row=mission_row),\n83\t        must_satisfy=self_change_must_satisfy(),\n84\t        allowed_scope=self_change_allowed_scope(),\n85\t    )\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tNEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS: dict[str, ContractBuilder] = {\n89\t    SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION: _self_change_contract,\n90\t}\n91\t\n92\tGENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS: dict[str, GenericProjection] = {\n93\t    ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION: GenericProjection(\n94\t        source_kind=ISSUE_MISSION_SOURCE_KIND,\n95\t        consumer=\"aria_kernel.plan_synthesizer:scan_github_issue_missions\",\n96\t        reason=(\n97\t            \"triage of an `aria`-labelled issue IS planner work: the planner reads the mission under the \"\n98\t            \"generic projection, and plan_synthesizer.scan_github_issue_missions reads the same mission as a \"\n99\t            \"ranked plan-candidate source (PlanCandidateSource.GITHUB_ISSUE); a structured contract would be a \"\n100\t            \"second copy of the planner's own question\"\n101\t        ),\n102\t    ),\n103\t}\n104\t\n105\t\n106\tdef contract_for_mission(*, mission_row: dict[str, Any], queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None) -> MissionContract | None:\n107\t    \"\"\"The contract this mission's pointer mints, or ``None`` for the generic queue projection.\"\"\"\n108\t    builder = NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS.get(str(mission_row.get(\"next_action\") or \"\"))\n109\t    if builder is None:\n110\t        return None\n111\t    return builder(queue_item_id=queue_item_id, source_cycle_id=source_cycle_id, mission_row=mission_row)\n112\t\n113\t\n114\tdef in_flight_mission_request(*, mission_id: str, contract: MissionContract, base_dir: str | Path | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n115\t    \"\"\"The request already asking this mission THIS contract, if one is still in flight.\n116\t\n117\t    In flight = its derived state is not in `TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES`.\n118\t    ACCEPTED and REJECTED are verdicts about the work and do NOT hold the\n119\t    mission: an accepted answer the kernel refused (an authority surface)\n120\t    leaves the mission DISCOVERED and re-selectable, which is the intended\n121\t    re-ask; an accepted answer the kernel honoured parks the mission in\n122\t    HUMAN_REQUIRED, which the scheduler skips. The result carries\n123\t    ``request_state`` so the drain's disclosure names what is holding it.\n124\t    \"\"\"\n125\t    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_state, list_agent_invocation_requests\n126\t    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n127\t\n128\t    marker = f\"mission:{mission_id}\"\n129\t    for request in reversed(list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir)):\n130\t        if str(request.get(\"pressure_event_id\") or \"\") != marker:\n131\t            continue\n132\t        asked = frozenset(str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in (request.get(\"must_satisfy\") or []) if isinstance(item, dict))\n133\t        if asked != contract.contract_ids:\n134\t            continue\n135\t        state = derive_request_state(request_id=str(request.get(\"request_id\")), base_dir=base_dir)\n136\t        if state in TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES:\n137\t            continue\n138\t        return {**request, \"request_state\": state}\n139\t    return None\n140\t\n141\t\n142\t__all__ = [\"GENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS\", \"GenericProjection\", \"NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS\", \"MissionContract\", \"contract_for_mission\",\n143\t           \"in_flight_mission_request\"]\n144\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"fcc42abd-ef39-48ec-8e1f-641ab68c8e52","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:35:32.551Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/mission_dispatch.py","content":"\"\"\"Which agent contract a mission's `next_action` pointer mints — the table the queue drain reads.\n\nWHY: a mission's forward pointer (`next_action`, ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730) was\nforwarded by the orchestrator's mission branch as FREE PROMPT TEXT\n(`recommended_action`) under one generic contract, whatever it said. Two\nkernel-owned pointers existed and neither was read by anyone:\n`SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION` (a self-change must reach `propose_self_change`\nwith structured fields) and `ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION` (a labelled GitHub\nissue). Confirmed live 2026-09-12.\n\nWHAT: two tables that PARTITION the kernel-minted pointers, the same shape\nas `gateway.default_schedules` (`DEFAULT_SCHEDULES` / `OPERATOR_ONLY_ACTIONS`):\n* `NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS` — pointers whose answer has a KERNEL consumer.\n  The builder returns the prompt, must_satisfy and allowed_scope the drain\n  mints; the accepted answer is bridged to that consumer.\n* `GENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS` — pointers for which the generic queue\n  projection IS the dispatch, each with the mandatory reason and the NAMED\n  consumer that reads the mission by its `source_kind`. A bare ``None`` in\n  the contract table used to say the same thing with no reason and no\n  consumer — an enumeration escape hatch a new pointer could hide in.\nA pointer in neither table, or in both, fails\n`tests/test_self_change_bridge.py::EveryNextActionHasADispatcher`, so a new\n`*_NEXT_ACTION` constant cannot ship minted-but-unread.\n\nA contract is asked ONCE PER MISSION while a request is in flight\n(`in_flight_mission_request`): the scheduler never moves a DISCOVERED\nmission and the queue de-duplicates per pending item, so without this rule\nevery cycle re-queued the same mission and the drain minted a second\nrequest for a question the first had not yet answered.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom .gateway.router import ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION, ISSUE_MISSION_SOURCE_KIND\nfrom .self_change_bridge import build_self_change_prompt, self_change_allowed_scope, self_change_must_satisfy\nfrom .self_improvement import SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION, is_self_change_mission\nfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass MissionContract:\n    prompt: dict[str, Any]\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]]\n    allowed_scope: list[str]\n\n    @property\n    def contract_ids(self) -> frozenset[str]:\n        \"\"\"The contract's identity: what `is_self_change_request` and the in-flight lookup compare.\"\"\"\n        return frozenset(str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in self.must_satisfy if isinstance(item, dict))\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass GenericProjection:\n    \"\"\"A pointer the generic queue projection dispatches — with the proof that someone reads the mission.\n\n    ``consumer`` is ``\"<module>:<function>\"``: a scanner that takes\n    ``workspace_root`` and returns candidate rows whose ``candidate_id`` is\n    the mission id, for missions of ``source_kind``. The invariant test\n    resolves it and runs it against a minted mission, so the entry cannot\n    name a consumer that does not exist or does not read this source kind.\n    \"\"\"\n    source_kind: str\n    consumer: str\n    reason: str\n\n\nContractBuilder = Callable[..., MissionContract]\n\n\ndef _self_change_contract(*, queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None, mission_row: dict[str, Any]) -> MissionContract:\n    if not is_self_change_mission(mission_row):\n        # The pointer alone is not the discriminator: a foreign source kind\n        # carrying it is a producer defect, disclosed at the mint rather\n        # than silently handed the generic contract.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"self_change_pointer_on_foreign_mission:{mission_row.get('mission_id')}:{mission_row.get('source_kind')}\"\n        )\n    return MissionContract(\n        prompt=build_self_change_prompt(queue_item_id=queue_item_id, source_cycle_id=source_cycle_id, mission_row=mission_row),\n        must_satisfy=self_change_must_satisfy(),\n        allowed_scope=self_change_allowed_scope(),\n    )\n\n\nNEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS: dict[str, ContractBuilder] = {\n    SELF_CHANGE_NEXT_ACTION: _self_change_contract,\n}\n\nGENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS: dict[str, GenericProjection] = {\n    ISSUE_MISSION_NEXT_ACTION: GenericProjection(\n        source_kind=ISSUE_MISSION_SOURCE_KIND,\n        consumer=\"aria_kernel.plan_synthesizer:scan_github_issue_missions\",\n        reason=(\n            \"triage of an `aria`-labelled issue IS planner work: the planner reads the mission under the \"\n            \"generic projection, and plan_synthesizer.scan_github_issue_missions reads the same mission as a \"\n            \"ranked plan-candidate source (PlanCandidateSource.GITHUB_ISSUE); a structured contract would be a \"\n            \"second copy of the planner's own question\"\n        ),\n    ),\n}\n\n\ndef contract_for_mission(*, mission_row: dict[str, Any], queue_item_id: str, source_cycle_id: str | None) -> MissionContract | None:\n    \"\"\"The contract this mission's pointer mints, or ``None`` for the generic queue projection.\"\"\"\n    builder = NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS.get(str(mission_row.get(\"next_action\") or \"\"))\n    if builder is None:\n        return None\n    return builder(queue_item_id=queue_item_id, source_cycle_id=source_cycle_id, mission_row=mission_row)\n\n\ndef in_flight_mission_request(*, mission_id: str, contract: MissionContract, base_dir: str | Path | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"The request already asking this mission THIS contract, if one is still in flight.\n\n    In flight = its derived state is not in `TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES`.\n    ACCEPTED and REJECTED are verdicts about the work and do NOT hold the\n    mission: an accepted answer the kernel refused (an authority surface)\n    leaves the mission DISCOVERED and re-selectable, which is the intended\n    re-ask; an accepted answer the kernel honoured parks the mission in\n    HUMAN_REQUIRED, which the scheduler skips. The result carries\n    ``request_state`` so the drain's disclosure names what is holding it.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_state, list_agent_invocation_requests\n    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n\n    marker = f\"mission:{mission_id}\"\n    for request in reversed(list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=base_dir)):\n        if str(request.get(\"pressure_event_id\") or \"\") != marker:\n            continue\n        asked = frozenset(str(item.get(\"id\")) for item in (request.get(\"must_satisfy\") or []) if isinstance(item, dict))\n        if asked != contract.contract_ids:\n            continue\n        state = derive_request_state(request_id=str(request.get(\"request_id\")), base_dir=base_dir)\n        if state in TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES:\n            continue\n        return {**request, \"request_state\": state}\n    return None\n\n\n__all__ = [\"GENERIC_PROJECTION_POINTERS\", \"GenericProjection\", \"NEXT_ACTION_CONTRACTS\", \"MissionContract\", \"contract_for_mission\",\n           \"in_flight_mission_request\"]\n","numLines":144,"startLine":1,"totalLines":144}}}

min(1.0, score * 10)) * 10)\n2468\t        else:\n2469\t            priority = 10 + rank\n2470\t        result = open_mission(\n2471\t            source_kind=\"service_hardening\",\n2472\t            source_id=project,\n2473\t            repo_hash=rh,\n2474\t            title=(\n2475\t                f\"Harden {project}: secure/performant/sustainable/testable/\"\n2476\t                f\"documented/correct (charter D1-D6)\"\n2477\t            ),\n2478\t            next_action=next_action,\n2479\t            wake_condition=wake_condition,\n2480\t            capability=\"service_hardening\",\n2481\t            priority=priority,\n2482\t            target_project=project,\n2483\t            base_dir=context.base_dir,\n2484\t        )\n2485\t        # The heal verdict comes from `open_mission`: a mission opened before\n2486\t        # the contract was required gets one installed on re-open, and one a\n2487\t        # HUMAN parked keeps the operator's sentence. from .next_cycle_queue import append_pending\n2524\t\n2525\t    decision = select_next_mission(base_dir=context.base_dir)\n2526\t    selected = decision.selected if decision.selected else None\n2527\t    queued = None\n2528\t    if selected:\n2529\t        mission_id = str(selected.get(\"mission_id\") or \"\")\n2530\t        queued_row = append_pending(\n2531\t            context.base_dir,\n2532\t An unchanged refusal\n            # re-appended every night is the same weather-reporting the\n            # closure gate was doing — measured at 4 identical rows per cycle\n            # on an evidence-free night, forever. A refusal whose reason or\n            # census CHANGED is a different fact and gets its own row.\n            #\n            # Nothing is hidden by the silence: this phase declares\n            # `state_key=\"service_mission_seed\"`, so the complete `refused`\n            # list below is projected into EVERY cycle's persisted state, and\n            # `newly_disclosed` says which of them the governance ledger\n            # learned tonight. The dedupe removes repetition from the\n            # audit trail, not the fact from the cycle record.\n            disclosure = append_tools_governance_once(\n                context.base_dir,\n                \"service_mission_refused\",\n                row,\n                claim_keys=(\n                    \"project\", \"reason\", \"core\", \"scoped_pressures\",\n                    \"changed_paths\", \"open_findings\",\n                    \"unattributed_changed_paths\", \"unattributed_open_findings\",\n                ),\n            )\n            refused.append({\n                **{k: v for k, v in row.items() if k != \"schema_version\"},\n                \"newly_disclosed\": bool(disclosure[\"appended\"]),\n            })\n            continue\n        next_action, wake_condition, contract_source = contract\n        if project in SERVICE_HARDENING_CORE:\n            priority = rank\n        elif centrality:\n            score = centrality.get(project, 0.0)\n            priority = 10 + int((1.0 - min(1.0, score * 10)) * 10)\n        else:\n            priority = 10 + rank\n        result = open_mission(\n            source_kind=\"service_hardening\",\n            source_id=project,\n            repo_hash=rh,\n            title=(\n                f\"Harden {project}: secure/performant/sustainable/testable/\"\n                f\"documented/correct (charter D1-D6)\"\n            ),\n            next_action=next_action,\n            wake_condition=wake_condition,\n            capability=\"service_hardening\",\n            priority=priority,\n            target_project=project,\n            base_dir=context.base_dir,\n        )\n        # The heal verdict comes from `open_mission`: a mission opened before\n        # the contract was required gets one installed on re-open, and one a\n        # HUMAN parked keeps the operator's sentence. This phase reports that\n        # verdict rather than re-deciding it — the seed phase and the ingest\n        # path would otherwise hold two copies of the same rule, and the copy\n        # here was the one that overwrote an operator (`heal_declined` names\n        # that case explicitly so a declined heal is visible, not silent).\n        seeded.append({\n            \"project\": project,\n            \"mission_id\": str(result.get(\"mission_id\")),\n            \"idempotent\": bool(result.get(\"idempotent\")),\n            \"scoped_pressures\": len(pressures_here),\n            \"priority\": priority,\n            \"centrality\": round(centrality.get(project, 0.0), 6) if centrality else None,\n            \"contract_source\": contract_source,\n            \"contract_healed\": bool(result.get(\"healed\")),\n            \"heal_declined\": result.get(\"heal_declined\"),\n        })\n    return {\n        \"status\": \"completed\",\n        \"seeded\": seeded,\n        \"refused\": refused,\n        \"core\": list(SERVICE_HARDENING_CORE),\n        \"evidence_backed\": evidence_backed,\n    }\n\n\ndef _phase_mission_selection(context: PhaseContext) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run the scheduler in the cycle, and hand the winner to the queue.\n\n    `select_next_mission` had exactly one caller — the operator CLI — so even\n    a fully seeded mission ledger would never move without a human. The\n    selected mission becomes a bounded-queue item (`mission:<id>` marker) the\n    autonomy drain resolves into an agent request; the non-selections stay\n    recorded by the scheduler itself, because \"why not\" is the half of the\n    decision an operator actually debugs.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .mission_scheduler import select_next_mission\n    from .next_cycle_queue import append_pending\n\n    decision = select_next_mission(base_dir=context.base_dir)\n    selected = decision.selected if decision.selected else None\n    queued = None\n    if selected:\n        mission_id = str(selected.get(\"mission_id\") or \"\")\n        queued_row = append_pending(\n            context.base_dir,\n            source_cycle_id=context.cycle_id,\n            pressure_id=f\"mission:{mission_id}\",\n            recommended_action=str(selected.get(\"next_action\") or selected.get(\"title\") or \"advance the mission\"),\n            candidate_tools=[],\n        )\n        queued = (queued_row or {}).get(\"queue_item_id\")\n    return {\n        \"status\": \"completed\",\n        \"outcome\": decision.outcome,\n        \"selected_mission\": (selected or {}).get(\"mission_id\"),\n        \"selected_project\": (selected or {}).get(\"target_project\"),\n        \"queue_item_id\": queued,\n        \"considered\": decision.considered,\n    }\n\n\ndef _phase_learning_post_evidence_closure(context: PhaseContext) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    return run_learning_post_evidence_closure(","numLines":150,"startLine":2400,"totalLines":3966}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0146LdQ5eNKyAK8Yrp3oSKGq","type":"tool_result","content":"2255\t    \"\"\"Turn the examination's targeting into durable service-hardening missions.\n2256\t\n2257\t    `cycle_service_examination` computes which services changed, their\n2258\t    downstream ripple, the owning agents and the per-service pressures — and\n2259\t    had ZERO consumers. `SERVICE_MAP.json` inventories every platform service\n2260\t    each cycle — and nobody read it. This phase is the first consumer of the\n2261\t    first and, through the core list, the intent of the second: the charter's\n2262\t    per-service hardening program (§5) finally has a producer.\n2263\t\n2264\t    Idempotent by mission identity: re-seeding a service folds into the\n2265\t    mission it already opened.\n2266\t\n2267\t    ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730 — AND WHAT THE MEASUREMENT ACTUALLY SAID. An earlier\n2268\t    revision of this docstring named this phase \"the producer of the paralysed\n2269\t    store: 28 mission events against 27 violations\". Re-measured on\n2270\t    2026-08-19: the store holds 5 ``opened`` events, ``pressure`` x2 and\n2271\t    ``shadow_run_summary`` x3 — every one from `mission.adopt_task_candidates`\n2272\t    and NONE from here, with one recorded ``mission_closure_violation`` row\n2273\t    naming those five. This phase had produced nothing live; the rule it\n2274\t    adopts is still the right one, but it is not the confession it claimed.\n2275\t\n2276\t    It mints ONLY what it can also drive: `_service_closure_contract` derives\n2277\t    the forward pointer from the service's own findings / pressures / diff,\n2278\t    and a service that yields none is disclosed as a governance refusal\n2279\t    rather than minted as a mission nothing can advance. Re-seeding also\n2280\t    HEALS — through `open_mission`, which installs a contract on any\n2281\t    contract-less mission it re-opens, so the rule lives at the mint for both\n2282\t    producers instead of being copied into each phase.\n2283\t    \"\"\"\n2284\t    from .mission import open_mission\n2285\t\n2286\t    exam = context.result(\"service_examination\")\n2287\t    exam = exam if isinstance(exam, dict) else {}\n2288\t    # The producer (`impact_graph.cycle_service_examination`) emits\n2289\t    # per_service_pressures as a LIST of {service, layer, pressures} groups\n2290\t    # in topological order — not a dict keyed by project. The first live\n2291\t    # cycle over this phase failed with \"'list' object has no attribute\n2292\t    # 'get'\" because the seeder assumed the dict shape its own test had\n2293\t    # invented (ORPHAN-HIGH-622). Normalize at the boundary; accept both\n2294\t    # shapes so a future producer change cannot re-break the seeder.\n2295\t    raw_per_service = exam.get(\"per_service_pressures\") or []\n2296\t    if isinstance(raw_per_service, dict):\n2297\t        per_service = raw_per_service\n2298\t    else:\n2299\t        per_service = {\n2300\t            str(group.get(\"service\")): (group.get(\"pressures\") or [])\n2301\t            for group in raw_per_service\n2302\t            if isinstance(group, dict) and group.get(\"service\")\n2303\t        }\n2304\t    order = exam.get(\"examination_order\") or []\n2305\t    evidence_backed = [\n2306\t        entry[\"project\"] for entry in order\n2307\t        if entry.get(\"changed_files\") or per_service.get(entry.get(\"project\"))\n2308\t    ]\n2309\t    targets: list[str] = list(dict.fromkeys(list(SERVICE_HARDENING_CORE) + evidence_backed))\n2310\t    rh = repo_hash(context.workspace_root)\n2311\t    # Kalibre Zekâ Z4b — evidence-backed missions get a centrality-scored\n2312\t    # priority BAND instead of enumeration order: PageRank over the cached\n2313\t    # dependency graph answers \"how much of the platform stands on this\n2314\t    # service\". CORE keeps its 0-3 band (the operator's floor is not\n2315\t    # re-litigated by a graph); evidence-backed land at 10 + bucket where a\n2316\t    # more-central service gets a LOWER (stronger) priority. Graph absent →\n2317\t    # empty scores → stable fallback to enumeration order within the band.\n2318\t    centrality: dict[str, float] = {}\n2319\t    # The path -> project map the EXAMINATION used to name these very targets.\n2320\t    # Attribution and centrality come out of ONE read of ONE cache on purpose:\n2321\t    # a second path->project reader can disagree with the reader that chose\n2322\t    # the targets, and that disagreement is not academic — the first version\n2323\t    # of this phase attributed with `service_dimension.service_for_path`,\n2324\t    # which cannot name 54 of this repository's 71 nx projects, so it wrote\n2325\t    # \"no derivable evidence\" rows about the exact paths that had selected\n2326\t    # those projects.\n2327\t    project_roots: dict[str, str] = {}\n2328\t    try:\n2329\t        from .calibrated_intelligence import pagerank\n2330\t        from .impact_graph import cached_service_analysis_order\n2331\t\n2332\t        # `cached_service_analysis_order` is keyword-only. The pre-730 call\n2333\t        # passed the root POSITIONALLY, so every invocation raised TypeError\n2334\t        # into the handler below and the Z4b band silently never applied —\n2335\t        # dead code wearing a live comment. TypeError is out of the handler\n2336\t        # for the same reason: an absent graph is OSError/ValueError, a bad\n2337\t        # call is a defect and must not be absorbed as \"graph absent\".\n2338\t        cache = cached_service_analysis_order(\n2339\t            workspace_root=context.workspace_root, base_dir=context.base_dir\n2340\t        )\n2341\t        project_roots = {\n2342\t            name: root\n2343\t            for name, root in (cache.get(\"project_roots\") or {}).items()\n2344\t            if isinstance(name, str) and isinstance(root, str) and root.strip()\n2345\t        }\n2346\t        centrality = pagerank(cache.get(\"dependencies\") or {})\n2347\t    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n2348\t        centrality = {}\n2349\t    from .impact_graph import project_for_path\n2350\t\n2351\t    diff = context.result(\"cycle_diff\")\n2352\t    changed_paths = [\n2353\t        path\n2354\t        for path in ((diff.get(\"changed_paths\") if isinstance(diff, dict) else None) or [])\n2355\t        if isinstance(path, str) and path.strip()\n2356\t    ]\n2357\t    changed_by_project: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n2358\t    for path in changed_paths:\n2359\t        owner = project_for_path(path, project_roots)\n2360\t        if owner:\n2361\t            changed_by_project.setdefault(owner, []).append(path)\n2362\t    # The open findings, grouped by PROJECT once. The paths come from\n2363\t    # `service_dimension.finding_dimension_paths` — the E15-c collector that\n2364\t    # mint-time and read-time both use, so the seeder cannot disagree with the\n2365\t    # findings ledger about which paths a finding cites — and the project\n2366\t    # comes from the same `project_for_path` the changed paths went through.\n2367\t    # Grouping here rather than filtering per project also keeps the ledger\n2368\t    # read at one pass instead of one per target service.\n2369\t    from .feedback_store import list_findings\n2370\t    from .service_dimension import finding_dimension_paths\n2371\t\n2372\t    open_findings = list_findings(status=\"open\", base_dir=context.base_dir)\n2373\t    findings_by_project: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n2374\t    for finding_row in open_findings:\n2375\t        finding_id = finding_row.get(\"finding_id\")\n2376\t        if not isinstance(finding_id, str) or not finding_id.strip():\n2377\t            continue\n2378\t        document = finding_row.get(\"finding\")\n2379\t        cited = finding_dimension_paths(document if isinstance(document, dict) else {})\n2380\t        for path in cited:\n2381\t            owner = project_for_path(path, project_roots)\n2382\t            if owner and finding_id not in findings_by_project.setdefault(owner, []):\n2383\t                findings_by_project[owner].append(finding_id)\n2384\t    seeded: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2385\t    refused: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2386\t    for rank, project in enumerate(targets):\n2387\t        pressures_here = per_service.get(project) or []\n2388\t        project_changed = changed_by_project.get(project) or []\n2389\t        project_findings = findings_by_project.get(project) or []\n2390\t        contract = _service_closure_contract(\n2391\t            project,\n2392\t            pressures=pressures_here,\n2393\t            changed_paths=project_changed,\n2394\t            finding_ids=project_findings,\n2395\t        )\n2396\t        if contract is None:\n2397\t            # The honest outcome: no evidence names a next action or a wake,\n2398\t            # so there is no mission to open. Disclosed rather than dropped —\n2399\t            # a service ARIA declined to harden is an operator-visible fact,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"2a92f292-560f-4d30-a5e3-da7c203643c6","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:35:43.072Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py","content":"    \"\"\"Turn the examination's targeting into durable service-hardening missions.\n\n    `cycle_service_examination` computes which services changed, their\n    downstream ripple, the owning agents and the per-service pressures — and\n    had ZERO consumers. `SERVICE_MAP.json` inventories every platform service\n    each cycle — and nobody read it. Re-seeding also\n    HEALS — through `open_mission`, which installs a contract on any\n    contract-less mission it re-opens, so the rule lives at the mint for both\n    producers instead of being copied into each phase.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .mission import open_mission\n\n    exam = context.result(\"service_examination\")\n    exam = exam if isinstance(exam, dict) else {}\n    # The producer (`impact_graph.cycle_service_examination`) emits\n    # per_service_pressures as a LIST of {service, layer, pressures} groups\n    # in topological order — not a dict keyed by project. The first live\n    # cycle over this phase failed with \"'list' object has no attribute\n    # 'get'\" because the seeder assumed the dict shape its own test had\n    # invented (ORPHAN-HIGH-622). Normalize at the boundary; accept both\n    # shapes so a future producer change cannot re-break the seeder.\n    raw_per_service = exam.get(\"per_service_pressures\") or []\n    if isinstance(raw_per_service, dict):\n        per_service = raw_per_service\n    else:\n        per_service = {\n            str(group.get(\"service\")): (group.get(\"pressures\") or [])\n            for group in raw_per_service\n            if isinstance(group, dict) and group.get(\"service\")\n        }\n    order = exam.get(\"examination_order\") or []\n    evidence_backed = [\n        entry[\"project\"] for entry in order\n        if entry.get(\"changed_files\") or per_service.get(entry.get(\"project\"))\n    ]\n    targets: list[str] = list(dict.fromkeys(list(SERVICE_HARDENING_CORE) + evidence_backed))\n    rh = repo_hash(context.workspace_root)\n    # Kalibre Zekâ Z4b — evidence-backed missions get a centrality-scored\n    # priority BAND instead of enumeration order: PageRank over the cached\n    # dependency graph answers \"how much of the platform stands on this\n    # service\". CORE keeps its 0-3 band (the operator's floor is not\n    # re-litigated by a graph); evidence-backed land at 10 + bucket where a\n    # more-central service gets a LOWER (stronger) priority. Graph absent →\n    # empty scores → stable fallback to enumeration order within the band.\n    centrality: dict[str, float] = {}\n    # The path -> project map the EXAMINATION used to name these very targets.\n    # Attribution and centrality come out of ONE read of ONE cache on purpose:\n    # a second path->project reader can disagree with the reader that chose\n    # the targets, and that disagreement is not academic — the first version\n    # of this phase attributed with `service_dimension.service_for_path`,\n    # which cannot name 54 of this repository's 71 nx projects, so it wrote\n    # \"no derivable evidence\" rows about the exact paths that had selected\n    # those projects.\n    project_roots: dict[str, str] = {}\n    try:\n        from .calibrated_intelligence import pagerank\n        from .impact_graph import cached_service_analysis_order\n\n        # `cached_service_analysis_order` is keyword-only. The pre-730 call\n        # passed the root POSITIONALLY, so every invocation raised TypeError\n        # into the handler below and the Z4b band silently never applied —\n        # dead code wearing a live comment. TypeError is out of the handler\n        # for the same reason: an absent graph is OSError/ValueError, a bad\n        # call is a defect and must not be absorbed as \"graph absent\".\n        cache = cached_service_analysis_order(\n            workspace_root=context.workspace_root, base_dir=context.base_dir\n        )\n        project_roots = {\n            name: root\n            for name, root in (cache.get(\"project_roots\") or {}).items()\n            if isinstance(name, str) and isinstance(root, str) and root.strip()\n        }\n        centrality = pagerank(cache.get(\"dependencies\") or {})\n    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        centrality = {}\n    from .impact_graph import project_for_path\n\n    diff = context.result(\"cycle_diff\")\n    changed_paths = [\n        path\n        for path in ((diff.get(\"changed_paths\") if isinstance(diff, dict) else None) or [])\n        if isinstance(path, str) and path.strip()\n    ]\n    changed_by_project: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for path in changed_paths:\n        owner = project_for_path(path, project_roots)\n        if owner:\n            changed_by_project.setdefault(owner, []).append(path)\n    # The open findings, grouped by PROJECT once. The paths come from\n    # `service_dimension.finding_dimension_paths` — the E15-c collector that\n    # mint-time and read-time both use, so the seeder cannot disagree with the\n    # findings ledger about which paths a finding cites — and the project\n    # comes from the same `project_for_path` the changed paths went through.\n    # Grouping here rather than filtering per project also keeps the ledger\n    # read at one pass instead of one per target service.\n    from .feedback_store import list_findings\n    from .service_dimension import finding_dimension_paths\n\n    open_findings = list_findings(status=\"open\", base_dir=context.base_dir)\n    findings_by_project: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for finding_row in open_findings:\n        finding_id = finding_row.get(\"finding_id\")\n        if not isinstance(finding_id, str) or not finding_id.strip():\n            continue\n        document = finding_row.get(\"finding\")\n        cited = finding_dimension_paths(document if isinstance(document, dict) else {})\n        for path in cited:\n            owner = project_for_path(path, project_roots)\n            if owner and finding_id not in findings_by_project.setdefault(owner, []):\n                findings_by_project[owner].append(finding_id)\n    seeded: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    refused: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for rank, project in enumerate(targets):\n        pressures_here = per_service.get(project) or []\n        project_changed = changed_by_project.get(project) or []\n        project_findings = findings_by_project.get(project) or []\n        contract = _service_closure_contract(\n            project,\n            pressures=pressures_here,\n            changed_paths=project_changed,\n            finding_ids=project_findings,\n        )\n        if contract is None:\n            # The honest outcome: no evidence names a next action or a wake,\n            # so there is no mission to open. Disclosed rather than dropped —\n            # a service ARIA declined to harden is an operator-visible fact,","numLines":145,"startLine":2255,"totalLines":3966}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CMQXnyqxkcMNTryBEJMvKK","type":"tool_result","content":"2125\t\n2126\t\n2127\t# The four core services, in the risk order the service-audit program set\n2128\t# (charter M-5.1). The list sets the ORDER and the priority floor of the\n2129\t# hardening program; every other service earns its place from examination\n2130\t# evidence (changed files or scoped pressures), so the mission ledger grows\n2131\t# with reality instead of opening 17 parallel fronts on day one.\n2132\t#\n2133\t# ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730 REWROTE WHAT MEMBERSHIP BUYS, AND WHAT IT STILL OWES.\n2134\t# Core services used to be MINTED even on a quiet night: with no findings, no\n2135\t# pressures and no diff there is nothing to name as a next action and nothing\n2136\t# to name as a wake, so the mint would produce exactly the row the closure\n2137\t# gate exists to report. (The live store's 5 paralysed rows came from the\n2138\t# ingest path, not from here — this phase had never produced one. The rule is\n2139\t# right regardless of which producer proved it.)\n2140\t#\n2141\t# What membership buys now: a core service is CONSIDERED first and priced\n2142\t# lowest. What it still owes, and what charter M-5.1 actually demands, is that\n2143\t# a core service is NEVER SILENTLY SKIPPED — so on a quiet night each of the\n2144\t# four is disclosed as a `service_mission_refused` row carrying ``core: true``\n2145\t# rather than minted or dropped. The successor pin\n2146\t# `test_core_services_are_considered_and_disclosed_on_a_quiet_night` holds\n2147\t# that obligation; the disclosure is what keeps \"ARIA declined to harden\n2148\t# auth-service tonight\" distinguishable from \"ARIA never looked at it\".\n2149\tSERVICE_HARDENING_CORE: tuple[str, ...] = (\n2150\t    \"auth-service\", \"billing-service\", \"farm-service\", \"sensor-service\",\n2151\t)\n2152\t\n2153\t\n2154\tdef _service_closure_contract(\n2155\t    project: str,\n2156\t    *,\n2157\t    pressures: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2158\t    changed_paths: list[str],\n2159\t    finding_ids: list[str],\n2160\t) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any], str] | None:\n2161\t    \"\"\"The forward pointer for a service-hardening mission, or ``None``.\n2162\t\n2163\t    ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730 — `mission.open_mission` now refuses a mint without\n2164\t    ``next_action`` + ``wake_condition``, so this function is what decides\n2165\t    whether the service HAS a mission at all. Every branch names a real\n2166\t    identifier the seeded service actually owns; nothing here composes a\n2167\t    stand-in, because a mission whose wake key names no evidence is exactly\n2168\t    the row the closure gate exists to report.\n2169\t\n2170\t    ATTRIBUTION DOES NOT HAPPEN HERE. ``changed_paths`` and ``finding_ids``\n2171\t    arrive already attributed to THIS project by the caller, through\n2172\t    `impact_graph.project_for_path` — the same longest-root-prefix rule that\n2173\t    produced the nx project names this function is handed. The first version\n2174\t    asked `service_dimension.service_for_path` instead: that vocabulary emits\n2175\t    ``shared:<lib>`` / ``web:<app>`` / ``None`` and can never produce 54 of\n2176\t    this repository's 71 nx project names, so for those 54 the seeder wrote a\n2177\t    governance row claiming no derivable evidence about the very paths that\n2178\t    had just selected the project. Two path -> project vocabularies deciding\n2179\t    whether a mission exists is one more than the number of correct answers.\n2180\t\n2181\t    THE TIER ORDER IS READ OFF `mission_scheduler.SOURCE_RANK` — literally,\n2182\t    not in prose. That table is this repo's statement of evidence authority\n2183\t    (a confirmed finding outranks a pressure); a changed path is evidence but\n2184\t    not a mission source at all, so it ranks strictly below every source that\n2185\t    is. Reordering SOURCE_RANK reorders these tiers with it instead of\n2186\t    leaving two orders to drift apart.\n2187\t\n2188\t    Returns ``(next_action, wake_condition, contract_source)``.\n2189\t    \"\"\"\n2190\t    from .mission_scheduler import SOURCE_RANK\n2191\t\n2192\t    # (rank, contract_source, next_action, wake_condition)\n2193\t    candidates: list[tuple[int, str, str, dict[str, Any]]] = []\n2194\t\n2195\t    # A confirmed open defect this service owns. Ordered by finding_id\n2196\t    # alone: two severity vocabularies live in this kernel\n2197\t    # (`finding.SEVERITY_RANK` is uppercase INFORMATIONAL..HIGH, tool\n2198\t    # findings carry lowercase critical..low), so choosing one to sort by\n2199\t    # here would fork it. The action names the COUNT and the agent triages.\n2200\t    findings = sorted(set(finding_ids))\n2201\t    if findings:\n2202\t        candidates.append((\n2203\t            SOURCE_RANK[\"finding\"],\n2204\t            \"open_finding\",\n2205\t            f\"Harden {project} against its {len(findings)} open finding(s), \"\n2206\t            f\"starting with {findings[0]}\",\n2207\t            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"finding:{findings[0]}\"},\n2208\t        ))\n2209\t\n2210\t    # A pressure this cycle scoped onto the service. Only a pressure that\n2211\t    # carries its own id qualifies: an id-less pressure would force a key\n2212\t    # naming the service instead of the evidence, and every such mission\n2213\t    # would then share one handle.\n2214\t    identified = sorted(\n2215\t        (\n2216\t            str(pressure.get(\"pressure_id\"))\n2217\t            for pressure in pressures\n2218\t            if isinstance(pressure, dict)\n2219\t            and isinstance(pressure.get(\"pressure_id\"), str)\n2220\t            and pressure[\"pressure_id\"].strip()\n2221\t        )\n2222\t    )\n2223\t    if identified:\n2224\t        candidates.append((\n2225\t            SOURCE_RANK[\"pressure\"],\n2226\t            \"scoped_pressure\",\n2227\t            f\"Examine {project} against the {len(identified)} pressure(s) scoped \"\n2228\t            f\"to it this cycle, starting with {identified[0]}\",\n2229\t            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"pressure:{identified[0]}\"},\n2230\t        ))\n2231\t\n2232\t    # The service moved this cycle. A changed path is evidence but not a\n2233\t    # mission source, so it cannot appear in SOURCE_RANK at all; ranking it\n2234\t    # one step below the weakest ranked source keeps it last WITHOUT pinning\n2235\t    # a number that a future source could quietly overtake.\n2236\t    mine = sorted(set(changed_paths))\n2237\t    if mine:\n2238\t        candidates.append((\n2239\t            max(SOURCE_RANK.values()) + 1,\n2240\t            \"changed_path\",\n2241\t            f\"Review the {len(mine)} path(s) changed in {project} this cycle \"\n2242\t            f\"against charter D1-D6, starting with {mine[0]}\",\n2243\t            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"changed_path:{mine[0]}\"},\n2244\t        ))\n2245\t\n2246\t    if not candidates:\n2247\t        return None\n2248\t    _, contract_source, next_action, wake_condition = min(\n2249\t        candidates, key=lambda candidate: (candidate[0], candidate[1])\n2250\t    )\n2251\t    return next_action, wake_condition, contract_source\n2252\t\n2253\t\n2254\tdef _phase_service_mission_seed(context: PhaseContext) -> dict[str, Any]:"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bf14544-6842-4df2-a4f5-61e2b4a46153","uuid":"58c038da-1278-4ba8-a094-ab1c103e79aa","timestamp":"2026-09-19T13:35:48.558Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-autonomy-planner-becc7f34b380/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py","content":"\n\n# The four core services, in the risk order the service-audit program set\n# (charter M-5.1). The list sets the ORDER and the priority floor of the\n# hardening program; every other service earns its place from examination\n# evidence (changed files or scoped pressures), so the mission ledger grows\n# with reality instead of opening 17 parallel fronts on day one.\n#\n# ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730 REWROTE WHAT MEMBERSHIP BUYS, AND WHAT IT STILL OWES.\n# Core services used to be MINTED even on a quiet night: with no findings, no\n# pressures and no diff there is nothing to name as a next action and nothing\n# to name as a wake, so the mint would produce exactly the row the closure\n# gate exists to report. (The live store's 5 paralysed rows came from the\n# ingest path, not from here — this phase had never produced one. The rule is\n# right regardless of which producer proved it.)\n#\n# What membership buys now: a core service is CONSIDERED first and priced\n# lowest. What it still owes, and what charter M-5.1 actually demands, is that\n# a core service is NEVER SILENTLY SKIPPED — so on a quiet night each of the\n# four is disclosed as a `service_mission_refused` row carrying ``core: true``\n# rather than minted or dropped. The successor pin\n# `test_core_services_are_considered_and_disclosed_on_a_quiet_night` holds\n# that obligation; the disclosure is what keeps \"ARIA declined to harden\n# auth-service tonight\" distinguishable from \"ARIA never looked at it\".\nSERVICE_HARDENING_CORE: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"auth-service\", \"billing-service\", \"farm-service\", \"sensor-service\",\n)\n\n\ndef _service_closure_contract(\n    project: str,\n    *,\n    pressures: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    changed_paths: list[str],\n    finding_ids: list[str],\n) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any], str] | None:\n    \"\"\"The forward pointer for a service-hardening mission, or ``None``.\n\n    ORPHAN-MEDIUM-730 — `mission.open_mission` now refuses a mint without\n    ``next_action`` + ``wake_condition``, so this function is what decides\n    whether the service HAS a mission at all. Every branch names a real\n    identifier the seeded service actually owns; nothing here composes a\n    stand-in, because a mission whose wake key names no evidence is exactly\n    the row the closure gate exists to report.\n\n    ATTRIBUTION DOES NOT HAPPEN HERE. ``changed_paths`` and ``finding_ids``\n    arrive already attributed to THIS project by the caller, through\n    `impact_graph.project_for_path` — the same longest-root-prefix rule that\n    produced the nx project names this function is handed. The first version\n    asked `service_dimension.service_for_path` instead: that vocabulary emits\n    ``shared:<lib>`` / ``web:<app>`` / ``None`` and can never produce 54 of\n    this repository's 71 nx project names, so for those 54 the seeder wrote a\n    governance row claiming no derivable evidence about the very paths that\n    had just selected the project. Two path -> project vocabularies deciding\n    whether a mission exists is one more than the number of correct answers.\n\n    THE TIER ORDER IS READ OFF `mission_scheduler.SOURCE_RANK` — literally,\n    not in prose. That table is this repo's statement of evidence authority\n    (a confirmed finding outranks a pressure); a changed path is evidence but\n    not a mission source at all, so it ranks strictly below every source that\n    is. Reordering SOURCE_RANK reorders these tiers with it instead of\n    leaving two orders to drift apart.\n\n    Returns ``(next_action, wake_condition, contract_source)``.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .mission_scheduler import SOURCE_RANK\n\n    # (rank, contract_source, next_action, wake_condition)\n    candidates: list[tuple[int, str, str, dict[str, Any]]] = []\n\n    # A confirmed open defect this service owns. Ordered by finding_id\n    # alone: two severity vocabularies live in this kernel\n    # (`finding.SEVERITY_RANK` is uppercase INFORMATIONAL..HIGH, tool\n    # findings carry lowercase critical..low), so choosing one to sort by\n    # here would fork it. The action names the COUNT and the agent triages.\n    findings = sorted(set(finding_ids))\n    if findings:\n        candidates.append((\n            SOURCE_RANK[\"finding\"],\n            \"open_finding\",\n            f\"Harden {project} against its {len(findings)} open finding(s), \"\n            f\"starting with {findings[0]}\",\n            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"finding:{findings[0]}\"},\n        ))\n\n    # A pressure this cycle scoped onto the service. Only a pressure that\n    # carries its own id qualifies: an id-less pressure would force a key\n    # naming the service instead of the evidence, and every such mission\n    # would then share one handle.\n    identified = sorted(\n        (\n            str(pressure.get(\"pressure_id\"))\n            for pressure in pressures\n            if isinstance(pressure, dict)\n            and isinstance(pressure.get(\"pressure_id\"), str)\n            and pressure[\"pressure_id\"].strip()\n        )\n    )\n    if identified:\n        candidates.append((\n            SOURCE_RANK[\"pressure\"],\n            \"scoped_pressure\",\n            f\"Examine {project} against the {len(identified)} pressure(s) scoped \"\n            f\"to it this cycle, starting with {identified[0]}\",\n            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"pressure:{identified[0]}\"},\n        ))\n\n    # The service moved this cycle. A changed path is evidence but not a\n    # mission source, so it cannot appear in SOURCE_RANK at all; ranking it\n    # one step below the weakest ranked source keeps it last WITHOUT pinning\n    # a number that a future source could quietly overtake.\n    mine = sorted(set(changed_paths))\n    if mine:\n        candidates.append((\n            max(SOURCE_RANK.values()) + 1,\n            \"changed_path\",\n            f\"Review the {len(mine)} path(s) changed in {project} this cycle \"\n            f\"against charter D1-D6, starting with {mine[0]}\",\n            {\"kind\": \"evidence\", \"key\": f\"changed_path:{mine[0]}\"},\n        ))\n\n    if not candidates:\n        return None\n    _, contract_source, next_action, wake_condition = min(\n        candidates, key=lambda candidate: (candidate[0], candidate[1])\n    )\n    return next_action, wake_condition, contract_source\n\n\ndef _phase_service_mission_seed(context: PhaseContext) -> dict[str, Any]:","numLines":130,"startLine":2125,"totalLines":3966}}}
